readings of the key-off clock E when the old segment had PASSED its target on the previous clock (v = the value at E-1):
  A oldStep = one more step of the passed segment (its increment, its direction; the model)   B nextSeg = the next segment's step (what clock N+1 does)
  C noStep = hold on E, release from the next clock   D relStep = the release increment on E.   Elsewhere every reading is the established old-step rule.
